1. Use continuous/parametric optimization: Assume a_j = φ(t_j; θ) where φ is simple smooth function (e.g., exponential, power, sigmoid, polynomial, spline). Multiplicative update algorithm:
Input a normalized sum=1.
For it in range(max_iters):
  b=full_conv(a); M=max(b); J=2nM.
  Choose weights w:
    - softmax(beta*(b - M))? We want emphasize peaks. beta maybe increasing.
    - Or active: w = 1 top K, zero else.
  C = cross_corr_p_a(w,n) (gradient/2).
  If C has zeros, handle.
  Update a_new = a * (c / C)^γ where c = median(C) or mean, clip to [a*0.01, a*100]? Normalize.
  Line search: try fractions maybe γ values [0.1,0.2,0.5,1.0] and pick J minimization. This is like mirror descent.
Need ensure nonnegative. If C_i=0, factor inf; set C_i=eps.
This update reduces lags with high w by adjusting a_i proportional to compatibility C_i. Good.
We can also update using `a_new = a * np.exp(-η * C / C_mid)` then normalize. Similar.
We can run with beta annealing from 1 to 1000, γ small. Random restarts.

This is cheap: each iteration one FFT conv plus one FFT cross (but C uses w length 2n-1 and a; can compute with FFT). 2 FFTs of length 1536; fast. Could run thousands.

If B0>0. We can choose B_k to minimize max while satisfying B_k equals convolution of a for k≥n? Maybe we can solve for A directly by optimizing B0..? Another way: Start with A, compute B, project B to max constraint, factor to A, iterate. This is alternating projection between:

Let's implement function `multiplicative_optimize(a0, max_iters, deadline, tracker, beta_schedule)` top-level.
Inside, maintain a normalized. For each beta in schedule:
  for it in range(steps):
    b=fast_conv(a); M=b.max(); current=2nM
    p = softmax(beta*(b - M)) (stable). (weights concentrate near max).
    C = cross_corr_weights_a(p,a)
    C_safe = np.maximum(C, 1e-12 * a.sum()? maybe eps)
    base = np.median(C_safe)
    for eta in [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.05?]:
       a_try = a * np.exp(-eta * (C_safe / base - 1))  # mean log 1
       a_try = np.maximum(a_try,0); normalize.
       if finite and J < current - 1e-12 accept.
    if no accept, try eta smaller 0.01, or random perturb.
  update tracker.

Let's implement `gn_polish(a, max_iters, deadline, tracker, top_frac=0.5, ridge=1e-3)`.
At each iter:
 b=conv(a); M=b.max()
 S = indices where b > M*(1 - 0.05) maybe plus top L. L= min(n, max(32, int(0.5*n))). Sort order = np.argsort(b)[::-1][:L].
 r = b[order] - M_target. What M_target? Use current M maybe; then residuals for max zero, others negative. To reduce max, we can set M_target = M*(1 - 0.005) - 1e-12 and minimize residuals; but LS may not reduce all. Better define residual = b[order] - np.mean(b[order]) to equalize; line search on J. Equalization tends to lower max if mean < max. Let's use r = b[order] - M (M=current max). Desired to increase? Negative residuals; LS will try to increase them (to M), not decrease max. We want set them to lower M_target <M. Choose M_target = M - step_delta? But if too low infeasible. We can choose M_target = M*(1 - delta) with delta=0.005. Let residual = b - M_target. Let's derive normalized gradient properly.
We have F(x)=f(y(x)) where y=x/s, s=sum x. Scale invariant f(y) depends on y. We want gradient wrt x. For any variation δx, δy = (δx/s) - (y (δx·x)/s^2). Gradient wrt y = G (same dimension). Then derivative = G·δy = (G - (G·y) y)·δx / s. Because δx/s - y (x·δx)/s^2. Let H=(G - (G·y) y)/s. So gradient = (G - projection onto y? actually G - (G·y)y)? divided by s. If f homogeneous degree 0, G·y may be 0 if f invariant under scaling y? But G from smooth g normalized? For f=2n g(y), G homogeneous? y simplex, G may not have G·y=0, but derivative of f y along scaling zero; G·y should be 0 if y changed by scaling? Since y already normalized, scaling not variable. In formula, H=(G - y*(G·y))/s. Good.
Then add tiny (eps*(s-1)) gradient to avoid scale drift.
However `smooth_g_grad` returns gradient of g w.r.t sequence values y (sum1). It may not satisfy G·y=0, but projection handles.
Alternatively avoid normalized L-BFGS and use unnormalized parent to reduce complexity. But normalized with projection is better.

def propose_candidate(seed=42,budget_s=1500):
    t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+budget_s-20
    tracker={'best':None,'score':inf,'t0':t0}
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    parent=None
    try: load height_sequence_1
    if parent: update from parent
    lengths=[512, 768, 1024]
    # ensure parent length included maybe if >512
    if parent is not None and parent.size not in lengths: insert parent.size
    lengths=sorted(set(l for l in lengths if 64<=l<=2048))
    found=[]
    # Phase 1: Spline param search for each length maybe limited
    for n in lengths:
       if time>deadline: break
       p = spline_param_search(n, rng, K=min(64, max(24,n//8)), time_budget=..., tracker=tracker)
       if p: found.append(p)
    # Phase 2: one-harmonic search for n in lengths top 2
    for n in lengths[:2]:
       p=one_harmonic_search(n, rng, max_evals=3000, m_count=3, deadline)
       if p: found.append(p)
    # Add parent resampled and perturbations

       add_candidate
    # Phase 4: L-BFGS on top few (best overall and maybe from large n)

    for a in top:

    # Phase 5: LP mirror descent on best and maybe top 2

    # Phase 6: final polish while time: alternate multiplicative/LP/GN

    a = lp_mirror_descent(best, min(500, remaining*20), deadline, tracker)
    # Maybe resample best to larger length 1536 and multiplicative if time
    if time remaining > 120:
       for nlarge in [1536,2048]:
          a=resample(best,nlarge)
          a=multiplicative...
          a=lbfgs short?
    return best_list

Spline param search implementation details:
```
def spline_smooth_value(params, x, knots, beta):
    y_interp = np.interp(x, knots, params, left=params[0], right=params[-1])
    p = np.logaddexp(0.0, y_interp)  # softplus
    s=p.sum()
    if s<1e-12: return 1e10
    p=p/s
    b=fast_conv(p)
    bmax=b.max(); shift=beta*(b-bmax); z=np.exp(np.clip(shift,-50,50)).sum()
    return float(2*len(p)*(bmax+np.log(z))/beta)

Multiplicative optimize implementation details:
```
def multiplicative_optimize(a0, max_iters, deadline, tracker, betas=(2,10,50,200), top_frac=0.2):
    a=np.maximum(a0,0); s=a.sum(); if s<... return a0
    a=a/s
    n=len(a); current=compute_J(a); tracker_update(tracker,a)
    for beta in betas:
       if time>deadline: break
       for it in range(max_iters//len(betas)):
          b=fast_conv(a); M=b.max(); J=2*n*M
          if not finite: break
          if J<current-1e-12: current=J; tracker_update; last_improve=it
          # weights: softmax(beta*(b-M)) plus top active mix
          shift=beta*(b-M); p=np.exp(clip(shift)); p/=p.sum()
          # top active: indices b >= M*(1-top_tol) or top L
          # mix p=0.5*p+0.5*top
          # cross
          C=cross_corr_weights_a(p,a)
          C=np.maximum(C, 1e-15)
          base=np.median(C)
          if base<=0: break
          accepted=False
          for eta in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.02):
              logfac = -eta*(C/base - 1.0)
              a_try=a*np.exp(np.clip(logfac,-10,10))
              a_try=np.maximum(a_try,0); st=a_try.sum(); if st<... continue
              a_try/=st; J_try=compute_J(a_try)
              if J_try < current - 1e-13:
                  a=a_try; current=J_try; accepted=True; tracker_update; break
          if not accepted:
              # try tiny eta or random nudge
              eta=0.005; ... if not accept and time: perturb a += rng.normal(0,1e-3,n); normalize; current=compute_J; 
       # after beta, update
    return a
```

LP mirror descent: We can reuse parent code but optimize. Need `build_A_sparse` top-level. Use csc.
```
def build_A_sparse(a):
    n=len(a); m=2*n-1
    rows=[]; cols=[]; vals=[]
    for k in range(m):
       jl=max(0,k-n+1); jh=min(n-1,k)
       L=jh-jl+1
       rows.extend([k]*L)
       cols.extend(range(jl,jh+1))
       # indices k-j for j in jl..jh -> from k-jl down to k-jh
       idx = k - np.arange(jl,jh+1)
       vals.extend(a[idx].tolist())
    mat=csc_matrix((vals,(rows,cols)), shape=(m,n))
    return mat
```
`vals` extend with list from numpy maybe. Could be slow; use list comprehensions. For n=512 okay. For 1024 maybe. Could optimize by precomputing cols? But a changes. We'll stick.

def gn_polish(a0, max_iters, deadline, tracker, top_frac=0.25, delta=0.002):
   a=normalize; n; current=compute_J; tracker_update
   for it in range(max_iters):
      if time>deadline: break
      b=fast_conv(a); M=b.max()
      # choose active: top L where b > M*(1-0.02)
      order=np.argsort(b)[::-1]
      L=min(n, max(32, int(0.25*n)))
      S=order[:L]
      Mt=M*(1-delta)
      r=b[S]-Mt
      # build J row for each k in S
      Jmat=np.zeros((L,n))
      for idx,k in enumerate(S):
         if k < n: # k-i valid i=k..n-1?
             start=max(0, k-(n-1))? Wait rows formula: J_{k,i}=2 a_{k-i} if 0<=k-i<n. For i from max(0,k-n+1) to min(n-1,k). Values a[k-i]. We can fill slice.
             i_l=max(0,k-n+1); i_h=min(n-1,k)
             Jmat[idx,i_l:i_h+1]=2*a[k - np.arange(i_l,i_h+1)]
      # Augmented: add constraint row μ ones, RHS 0; ridge maybe
      mu=10.0
      Aaug=np.vstack([Jmat, mu*np.ones((1,n))])
      rang=np.concatenate([r, [0.0]])
      # solve least squares with small damping via adding lam I? Use np.linalg.lstsq.
      try:
         delta_vec, *_ = np.linalg.lstsq(Aaug, rang, rcond=None)
      except: break
      # step backtracking
      improved=False
      for step in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05):
         a_try=np.maximum(a+step*delta_vec,0); normalize; J_try=compute_J; if J_try<current-1e-12: accept
      if not improved:
         delta*=0.5 if >1e-4 else break
   return a
```

Let's test correctness of cross_corr_weights_a.
```
def cross_corr_weights_a(w,a):
    # w length 2n-1, a length n
    n=len(a); m=2*n-1
    L=1
    while L < n + m - 1: L <<=1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    fw=np.fft.rfft(w[::-1],L)
    h=np.fft.irfft(fa*fw,L)[:n+m-1]
    return h[m-n:m][::-1]
```
Check length: n+m-1=3n-2. Index slice m-n = n-1 to m=2n-1 exclusive length n. Good.
For p sum1, C length n.
In gradient smooth, C as above. Good.

def one_harmonic_search(n, rng, m_list=(1,2,3), a_count=20, phi_count=60, refine=True):
    best_a=None; best_J=inf; best_param=None
    js=np.arange(n)
    for m in m_list:
       theta=2*np.pi*m/n
       for a in np.linspace(0.0, 0.98, a_count):
          p_shape = 1 + a*np.cos(np.outer? Too large. Loop phi vectorized over phi maybe:

             if p.min()< -1e-9: continue

Spline param search details with top-level objective:
```
def spline_obj(theta, n, knots, xgrid, beta):
    y_interp=np.interp(xgrid, knots, theta, left=theta[0], right=theta[-1])
    p=np.logaddexp(0.0, y_interp)
    s=p.sum()
    if s<=1e-12: return 1e12
    p=p/s
    b=fast_conv(p)
    bmax=b.max()
    shift=beta*(b-bmax)
    shift=np.clip(shift,-50,50)
    z=np.exp(shift).sum()
    if z<1e-300: return 1e12
    return float(2*n*(bmax+np.log(z))/beta)
```
No gradient, finite diff.
`softplus` np.logaddexp(0,y) maybe for y large >700 overflows? np.logaddexp stable. y bounds ±20, p up to e^20 ~4.8e8, but normalized; p values may huge for endpoints but finite. FFT of p with values 1e8 could overflow? If p normalized by sum, values O(1) if only few huge? If all huge, sum huge but float maybe 4e8*512=2e11 okay. If y=20, p≈4e8, sum 2e11, normalized ~1e-9 except if all same (scale). Actually if all y=20 constant, p constant; normalized 1/n. No overflow. If some y=20 and others -20, p sum ~4e8, normalized peak ~1. Good. So bounds 20 okay.
Bounds in L-BFGS: [(-20,20)]*K.

def seq_to_spline_theta(a,n,K):
    knots=np.linspace(0,1,K); xgrid=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    p=n*np.asarray(a)
    p=np.maximum(p,1e-8)
    # approximate log softplus: if p<1, y≈log(p); else log(expm1(p)) but p ~O(1) maybe.
    y = np.log(p) # since softplus(y)≈e^y for y<0 and ≈y for positive? Not exact but initial
    return np.interp(knots, xgrid, y, left=y[0], right=y[-1])

In `compute_J`, `a = np.maximum(np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64),0.0)`. If s==0 return inf. `a=a/s`. `b=fast_conv(a)`. `return float(2.0*len(a)*b.max())`.

`cross_corr_weights_a(w,a)`: w can be shape (2n-1,). Ensure w sum maybe 1 but no need.
```
n=len(a); m=2*n-1; L=1
while L < n + m - 1: L <<=1
fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
fw=np.fft.rfft(w[::-1],L)
h=np.fft.irfft(fa*fw,L)[:n+m-1]
return h[m-n:m][::-1]
```
Check if m-n = n-1. For n=1? m=1, slice [0:1] reverse ok. n=1 trivial.

`smooth_g_grad(y,beta)`:
```
b=fast_conv(y)
bmax=float(b.max())
shift=beta*(b-bmax); shift=np.clip(shift,-50,50)
ez=np.exp(shift); Z=ez.sum()
if Z<1e-300:
    p=np.zeros_like(b); p[int(np.argmax(b))]=1.0
else: p=ez/Z
g=(bmax+np.log(max(Z,1e-300)))/beta
C=cross_corr_weights_a(p,y)
return g, 2*C
```
If y has zeros, C okay.

`one_harmonic_obj` top-level:
```
def one_harmonic_obj(x, n, js, m):
    a=float(x[0]); phi=float(x[1])
    if a<0 or a>1.0: return 1e6 + abs(min(0,a-1))*? 
    p=1.0 + a*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*js/n + phi)
    mn=p.min()
    if mn < -1e-7:
       return 1e6 + (mn+0.0)**2
    p=np.maximum(p,0.0)
    s=p.sum()
    if s<1e-12: return 1e12
    p=p/s
    return compute_J(p)
```

`one_harmonic_search` returns best seq or None. Use top list.
```
def one_harmonic_search(n, seed, m_list=(1,2,3), a_count=18, phi_count=48, deadline=None):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    js=np.arange(n)
    best_list=[]
    best_j=inf; best_a=None
    phis=np.linspace(0,2*np.pi,phi_count,endpoint=False)
    amps=np.linspace(0.0,0.98,a_count)
    for m in m_list:
       base=np.cos(2*np.pi*m*js/float(n))
       for av in amps:
          p=1+av[...,None]*np.cos(base[None,:]+phis) # shape (a? no)
```
Vectors: For each av, compute `p_all = 1 + av*np.cos(base[:,None]+phis[None,:])` shape n x phi_count; but n*phi_count memory 512*48=24k fine. Then loop over col. This vectorizes cos but not conv. Could do for each phi.
Simpler nested loops.
To check time, 18*48*3=2592, okay.

Spline search:
```
def spline_param_search(n, seed, parent_seq, K=40, deadline=None, max_calls=10):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    knots=np.linspace(0,1,K); xgrid=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    inits=[]
    inits.append(np.zeros(K))
    # low freq random
    for _ in range(3):
       t=np.linspace(0,1,K)
       amp=rng.uniform(0.5,2.0)
       freq=rng.uniform(1,3)
       phase=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
       theta=amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*freq*t+phase) + rng.uniform(-1,1)
       # smooth
       kernel=np.ones(3)/3; theta=np.convolve(theta,kernel,'same')
       inits.append(theta)
    # linear ramp
    inits.append(np.linspace(-1,1,K))
    inits.append(np.linspace(1,-1,K))
    if parent_seq is not None:
       p = resample_seq(parent_seq,n)
       p=np.maximum(p,0); p/=max(p.sum(),1e-12)
       y=np.log(np.maximum(p*n, 1e-6))
       theta=np.interp(knots, np.linspace(0,1,n), y, left=y[0], right=y[-1])
       inits.append(theta)
    best_theta=np.zeros(K); best_val=1e12; best_seq=None
    betas=[5,20,100]
    calls=0
    bounds=[(-18,18)]*K
    for bi,beta in enumerate(betas):
       reps = 3 if beta<=20 else 2
       for r in range(reps):
          if calls>=max_calls or (deadline and time.monotonic()>deadline): break
          if bi==0:
             theta0=inits[r%len(inits)]
          else:
             if r==0: theta0=best_theta
             else: theta0=best_theta + rng.normal(0,0.3,K)
          maxfun = 800 if n>800 else 1500
          try:
             res=minimize(spline_obj, theta0, args=(n,knots,xgrid,beta), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':80,'maxfun':maxfun,'ftol':1e-12})
             calls+=1
          except: continue
          # normalize res.x? compute value exact
          val_modal = res.fun
          # exact seq
          p = softplus(interp(res.x)); p/=p.sum(); J=compute_J(p)
          if val_modal<best_val or J<best_j:
             best_theta=res.x.copy(); best_val=val_modal; best_seq=p
          # also update exact best_seq if J better
    if best_seq is not None: return best_seq
    # fallback uniform
    return None
```
Need `softplus_interp(theta,n,knots,xgrid)` helper top-level.
```
def seq_from_spline(theta, n, knots, xgrid):
    y=np.interp(xgrid, knots, theta, left=theta[0], right=theta[-1])
    p=np.logaddexp(0.0,y)
    s=p.sum(); if s<... return None
    p=p/s; return p
```

Multiplicative optimize details: Need `top_local`? We'll pass seed for perturb.
```
def multiplicative_optimize(a0, max_iters, deadline, tracker, seed=1, betas=(5,20,100,500)):
    a=normalize(a0); n=len(a); rng=np.random.default_rng(seed+1)
    current=compute_J(a); update_tracker(tracker,a)
    if not finite: return a
    it_per_beta=max(10,max_iters//len(betas))
    for beta in betas:
       for it in range(it_per_beta):
          if deadline and time.monotonic()>deadline: break
          b=fast_conv(a); M=float(b.max()); current=2*n*M
          if not finite: break
          # weights
          shift=beta*(b-M); shift=np.clip(shift,-50,50); ez=np.exp(shift); p=ez/ez.sum()
          # top mix
          Ktop=max(8, min(n, int(0.12*n)))
          order=np.argsort(b)[::-1]
          top=np.zeros_like(b); top[order[:Ktop]]=1.0; top/=top.sum()
          p=0.5*p + 0.5*top
          C=cross_corr_weights_a(p,a)
          C=np.maximum(C, 1e-18)
          base=float(np.median(C))
          if base<=0 or not finite: break
          accepted=False
          for eta in (0.07,0.14,0.035,0.2):
             logf=-eta*(C/base - 1.0)
             logf=np.clip(logf,-8,8)
             at=a*np.exp(logf)
             at=np.maximum(at,0); s=at.sum()
             if s<1e-10: continue
             at/=s
             J=compute_J(at)
             if np.isfinite(J) and J < current - 1e-13:
                a=at; current=J; update_tracker; accepted=True; break
          if not accepted:
             # small perturb
             if rng.uniform()<0.3:
                at=a + rng.normal(0,0.01/n,n) # scale? n 512 -> 2e-5
                at=np.maximum(at,0); at/=at.sum(); J=compute_J(at)
                if J<current: a=at; current=J; update
             else:
                # try updating with eta 0 and small geometric? no
                # no improvement; continue maybe after few break
                pass
       # after beta, update
    return a
```
Perturb scale: a values ~1/n, noise 0.01/n small. Good.
Eta order: try moderate 0.14, 0.07, 0.035, 0.2. If current max near optimum, small eta needed. Add 0.01 maybe.
We can adapt top K: if beta high, top K smaller? fine.

```
def lp_mirror_descent(a0, max_iters, deadline, tracker):
    a=normalize; current=compute_J; update
    n=len(a); stall=0
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time>deadline: break
       b=fast_conv(a); M=float(b.max())
       g=lp_best_response(a,M)
       if g is None: break
       g=np.maximum(g,0); gs=g.sum(); if gs<1e-10: break
       g/=gs
       improved=False
       for t in (0.15,0.05,0.3,0.02,0.5):
          at=(1-t)*a + t*g; at=np.maximum(at,0); s=at.sum(); if s<... continue; at/=s
          J=compute_J(at)
          if J < current - 1e-13:
             a=at; current=J; improved=True; break
       if not improved:
          # geometric
          for t in (0.2,0.1):
             at=np.power(np.maximum(a,1e-15),1-t)*np.power(np.maximum(g,1e-15),t)
             ...
          if not improved:
             stall+=1
             if stall>=4: break
       else: stall=0
       if it%20==0: update_tracker
    update_tracker; return a
```

`gn_polish`: Need be careful with large `np.linalg.lstsq` each iter. Use top L maybe 128 to speed.
```
def gn_polish(a0, max_iters, deadline, tracker, top_frac=0.25, target_shrink=0.01):
    a=normalize; current=compute_J; update
    n=len(a)
    L=min(n, max(32,int(top_frac*n)))
    shrink=0.01
    stall=0
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time>deadline: break
       b=fast_conv(a); M=float(b.max())
       order=np.argsort(b)[::-1]
       S=order[:L]
       target=M*(1-shrink)
       r=b[S]-target
       Jmat=np.zeros((L,n), dtype=np.float64)
       for idx,k in enumerate(S):
          il=max(0,k-n+1); ih=min(n-1,k)
          if il<=ih:
             Jmat[idx,il:ih+1] = 2.0*a[k - np.arange(il, ih+1)]
       mu=5.0
       Aaug=np.vstack((Jmat, mu*np.ones((1,n))))
       rang=np.concatenate((r, [0.0]))
       try:
          dv, *_ = np.linalg.lstsq(Aaug, rang, rcond=None)
       except: break
       improved=False
       for step in (0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05):
          at=np.maximum(a+step*dv,0); s=at.sum(); if s<... continue; at/=s
          J=compute_J(at)
          if J<current-1e-12:
             a=at; current=J; improved=True; break
       if not improved:
          shrink = max(1e-4, shrink*0.5)
          stall+=1
          if stall>5: break
       else:
          shrink = min(0.05, shrink*1.2)
          stall=0
       if it%20==0: update_tracker
    update_tracker; return a
```
If target too low, Jmat LS may increase some; line search. Good.

def propose_candidate(seed=42,budget_s=1500):
    t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+max(120,budget_s-20)
    tracker={'best':None,'score':float('inf'),'t0':t0}
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    parent=None
    try:
       p=np.asarray(height_sequence_1,dtype=float)
       if p.ndim==1 and p.size>0:
          p=np.clip(p,0,1000); ps=p.sum(); if ps>0: parent=p/ps
          update_tracker(tracker,parent)
    except Exception: pass
    pool=[]
    if parent is not None: pool_add(pool,parent,20)
    # lengths
    lengths=[]
    for L in [512, 768, 1024]:
       if L not in lengths: lengths.append(L)
    if parent is not None:
       npar=parent.size
       if npar not in lengths:
          lengths.append(npar)
       lengths.sort()
    # Add resampled parent to lengths
    for n in lengths:
       if parent is not None:
          a=resample_seq(parent,n); pool_add(pool,a)
       else:
          # uniform
          pool_add(pool,np.ones(n)/n)
    # Phase spline param for some lengths

    # Add some random/trig? Spline covers.
    # Phase multiplicative on pool top

    pool.sort(key=lambda x: x[0]) # lambda not allowed? Rule no lambda. Need avoid lambda. Use `pool.sort()` if tuples sorted by first. Tuples (score, seq). Good.

```python
"""
Search for a nonnegative sequence minimizing
J(a) = 2*n*max(np.convolve(a,a))/(sum(a))**2.

The program uses several complementing strategies:
  1. Flexible positive spline parameterization optimized by L-BFGS-B on a
     smoothed log-sum-exp objective.
  2. One-harmonic analytic/numeric constructions.
  3. Multiplicative mirror-descent updates with peak-violation weights.
  4. Smooth full-sequence L-BFGS-B on a normalized scale-invariant objective.
  5. LP-based Frank-Wolfe / mirror descent refinement.
  6. Active-set Gauss-Newton polishing of the largest convolution coefficients.
  7. Multi-length resampling and final polishing.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import linprog, minimize
from scipy.sparse import csc_matrix


def clean_sequence(a):
    """Return a validated normalized nonnegative sequence, or None."""
    try:
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if a.ndim != 1 or a.size == 0:
        return None
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        return None
    return a / s


def fast_conv(a):
    """Full linear self-convolution by FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    m = 2 * n - 1
    L = 1
    while L < m:
        L <<= 1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    out = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, L)[:m]
    # Remove tiny negative roundoff.
    out[out < 0.0] = 0.0
    return out


def compute_J(a):
    """Scalar objective for a sequence (scale invariant, normalized sum=1)."""
    a = clean_sequence(a)
    if a is None:
        return float("inf")
    b = fast_conv(a)
    return float(2.0 * a.size * float(b.max()))


def cross_corr_weights_a(w, a):
    """
    C_i = sum_k w_k a_{k-i} for i=0..n-1, where w has length 2n-1.
    This is half the derivative of weighted convolution coefficients.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    m = 2 * n - 1
    if w.size != m:
        w = w[:m] if w.size >= m else np.pad(w, (0, m - w.size))
    need = n + m - 1
    L = 1
    while L < need:
        L <<= 1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], L)
    h = np.fft.irfft(fa * fw, L)[:need]
    return h[m - n:m][::-1]


def smooth_g_grad(y, beta):
    """
    g = logsumexp(beta * conv(y,y))/beta and gradient wrt y.
    Assumes y is a valid finite vector.
    """
    b = fast_conv(y)
    bmax = float(b.max())
    shift = beta * (b - bmax)
    shift = np.clip(shift, -50.0, 50.0)
    ez = np.exp(shift)
    Z = float(ez.sum())
    if Z < 1e-300:
        p = np.zeros_like(b)
        p[int(np.argmax(b))] = 1.0
        g = bmax
    else:
        p = ez / Z
        g = (bmax + float(np.log(Z))) / beta
    C = cross_corr_weights_a(p, y)
    grad = 2.0 * C
    return float(g), grad


def full_func(x, n, beta):
    """Normalized smooth objective for full-sequence L-BFGS."""
    x2 = np.maximum(np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64), 0.0)
    s = float(x2.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return 1e12
    y = x2 / s
    g, _ = smooth_g_grad(y, beta)
    lam = 1e-8
    return float(2.0 * n * g + lam * (s - 1.0) * (s - 1.0))


def full_jac(x, n, beta):
    """Gradient of full_func."""
    x2 = np.maximum(np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64), 0.0)
    s = float(x2.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.zeros(x2.shape, dtype=np.float64)
    y = x2 / s
    _, Gy = smooth_g_grad(y, beta)
    G = 2.0 * n * Gy
    # Chain rule for y = x / sum(x).
    Gy_dot_y = float(np.dot(Gy, y))
    grad = (G - y * Gy_dot_y) / s
    lam = 1e-8
    grad += 2.0 * lam * (s - 1.0)
    grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return grad


def update_tracker(tracker, a):
    """Update a dict tracker with best sequence and score."""
    a = clean_sequence(a)
    if a is None:
        return
    score = compute_J(a)
    if np.isfinite(score) and score < tracker["score"]:
        tracker["score"] = score
        tracker["best"] = a.copy()
        print(
            f"  new best: J={score:.9f} n={a.size} "
            f"t={time.monotonic()-tracker['t0']:.1f}s",
            flush=True,
        )


def pool_add(pool, a, max_size=12, tracker=None):
    """Add a candidate to a fixed-size pool of (score, sequence)."""
    a = clean_sequence(a)
    if a is None:
        return None
    score = compute_J(a)
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return None
    if tracker is not None:
        update_tracker(tracker, a)
    if len(pool) < max_size:
        pool.append((score, a.copy()))
    else:
        worst_idx = 0
        worst_score = pool[0][0]
        for i in range(1, len(pool)):
            if pool[i][0] > worst_score:
                worst_score = pool[i][0]
                worst_idx = i
        if score < worst_score - 1e-15:
            pool[worst_idx] = (score, a.copy())
    return score


def resample_seq(src, dest_n):
    """Resample a 1D sequence to another length by linear interpolation."""
    src = clean_sequence(src)
    if src is None:
        return None
    if src.size == dest_n:
        return src.copy()
    t_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, src.size)
    t_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, dest_n)
    out = np.interp(t_new, t_old, src, left=src[0], right=src[-1])
    return clean_sequence(out)


def one_harmonic_obj(x, n, js, m):
    """Objective for p_j = (1 + a cos(2pi m j/n + phi))/sum(...)."""
    a = float(x[0])
    phi = float(x[1])
    if a < 0.0 or a > 1.0:
        return 1e6 + abs(a)
    p = 1.0 + a * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * js / float(n) + phi)
    mn = float(np.min(p))
    if mn < -1e-7:
        return 1e6 + mn * mn
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        return 1e12
    p /= s
    return compute_J(p)


def one_harmonic_search(n, seed, deadline, m_list=(1, 2, 3, 4),
                        a_count=20, phi_count=48):
    """Small global search over first few single trigonometric modes."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    js = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    phis = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi, phi_count, endpoint=False)
    amps = np.linspace(0.0, 0.98, a_count)

    top = []
    best_seq = None
    best_j = float("inf")

    def push_candidate(j, a, m, av, phi):
        nonlocal best_seq, best_j
        if not np.isfinite(j):
            return
        if j < best_j:
            best_seq = a.copy()
            best_j = j
        cand = (j, a.copy(), (av, phi, m))
        if len(top) < 8:
            top.append(cand)
        else:
            wi = 0
            wv = top[0][0]
            for i in range(1, len(top)):
                if top[i][0] > wv:
                    wv = top[i][0]
                    wi = i
            if j < wv:
                top[wi] = cand

    for m in m_list:
        base = np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * js / float(n))
        for av in amps:
            for idx, phi in enumerate(phis):
                if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
                    break
                p = 1.0 + av * (base * np.cos(phi) - np.sin(base) * np.sin(phi))
                if float(np.min(p)) < -1e-7:
                    continue
                p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
                s = float(p.sum())
                if s < 1e-12:
                    continue
                p /= s
                j = compute_J(p)
                push_candidate(j, p, m, av, phi)
            if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
                break
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

    # Refine a few best grid points.
    if top:
        used = []
        attempts = 0
        while len(used) < min(4, len(top)) and attempts < len(top):
            attempts += 1
            bi = -1
            bv = float("inf")
            for i, cand in enumerate(top):
                if i in used:
                    continue
                if cand[0] < bv:
                    bv = cand[0]
                    bi = i
            if bi < 0:
                break
            used.append(bi)
            j0, a0, params = top[bi]
            x0 = np.array([float(params[0]), float(params[1])], dtype=np.float64)
            m = int(params[2])
            try:
                res = minimize(
                    one_harmonic_obj,
                    x0,
                    args=(n, js, m),
                    method="Nelder-Mead",
                    options={"maxiter": 180, "xatol": 1e-5, "fatol": 1e-10},
                )
                if res is not None and res.fun < j0:
                    av, phi = float(res.x[0]), float(res.x[1])
                    p = 1.0 + av * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * js / float(n) + phi)
                    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
                    s = float(p.sum())
                    if s > 1e-12:
                        p /= s
                        jn = compute_J(p)
                        if np.isfinite(jn) and jn < j0:
                            push_candidate(jn, p, m, av, phi)
            except Exception:
                pass

    if best_seq is not None:
        return clean_sequence(best_seq)
    return None


def spline_obj(theta, n, knots, xgrid, beta):
    """Smoothed objective for softplus spline parameterization."""
    y = np.interp(xgrid, knots, theta, left=theta[0], right=theta[-1])
    p = np.logaddexp(0.0, y)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        return 1e12
    p /= s
    b = fast_conv(p)
    bmax = float(b.max())
    sh = beta * (b - bmax)
    sh = np.clip(sh, -50.0, 50.0)
    ez = np.exp(sh)
    Z = float(ez.sum())
    if Z < 1e-300:
        return 1e12
    return float(2.0 * n * (bmax + float(np.log(Z))) / beta)


def seq_from_spline(theta, n, knots, xgrid):
    """Convert spline parameters to an exact discrete sequence."""
    y = np.interp(xgrid, knots, theta, left=theta[0], right=theta[-1])
    p = np.logaddexp(0.0, y)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s < 1e-12:
        return None
    p /= s
    return p


def spline_param_search(n, seed, parent_seq, K=40, deadline=None, max_calls=8):
    """
    Optimize a positive softplus piecewise-linear density with L-BFGS-B.
    Returns the best discrete sequence found.
    """
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    knots = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, K)
    xgrid = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    bounds = [(-18.0, 18.0)] * K

    inits = []
    inits.append(np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64))

    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, K)
    for _ in range(4):
        amp = rng.uniform(0.5, 2.0)
        freq = rng.uniform(0.8, 3.0)
        phase = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        th = amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * freq * t + phase) + rng.normal(0.0, 0.35, K)
        th = np.convolve(th, np.ones(3.0) / 3.0, mode="same")
        th -= float(np.mean(th))
        inits.append(th)

    inits.append(np.linspace(-1.2, 1.2, K, dtype=np.float64))
    inits.append(np.linspace(1.2, -1.2, K, dtype=np.float64))

    if parent_seq is not None:
        p = resample_seq(parent_seq, n)
        if p is not None:
            p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
            s = float(p.sum())
            if s > 0:
                p /= s
                # Approximate log-density; softplus is close enough for init.
                y = np.log(np.maximum(p * float(n), 1e-8))
                th = np.interp(knots, np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n), y,
                               left=y[0], right=y[-1])
                inits.append(th)

    best_theta = None
    best_seq = None
    best_j = float("inf")
    calls = 0

    def consider(res_x):
        nonlocal best_theta, best_seq, best_j
        if res_x is None:
            return
        res_x = np.nan_to_num(res_x, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        seq = seq_from_spline(res_x, n, knots, xgrid)
        if seq is None:
            return
        j = compute_J(seq)
        if np.isfinite(j) and j < best_j:
            best_j = j
            best_theta = res_x.copy()
            best_seq = seq.copy()

    betas = [5.0, 20.0, 100.0]
    maxfun = 900 if n > 800 else 1400
    maxiter = 70 if n > 800 else 90

    for bi, beta in enumerate(betas):
        reps = 3 if beta <= 20.0 else 2
        for r in range(reps):
            if calls >= max_calls:
                break
            if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
                break
            if bi == 0:
                th0 = inits[r % len(inits)].copy()
            else:
                if r == 0 and best_theta is not None:
                    th0 = best_theta.copy()
                elif best_theta is not None:
                    th0 = best_theta + rng.normal(0.0, 0.25, K)
                else:
                    th0 = inits[r % len(inits)].copy()
            try:
                res = minimize(
                    spline_obj,
                    th0,
                    args=(n, knots, xgrid, beta),
                    method="L-BFGS-B",
                    bounds=bounds,
                    options={"maxiter": maxiter, "maxfun": maxfun, "ftol": 1e-13},
                )
                calls += 1
                if res is not None and res.x is not None:
                    consider(res.x)
                    consider(th0)
            except Exception:
                calls += 1
                pass
        if best_theta is not None:
            consider(best_theta)
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

    if best_seq is not None:
        return clean_sequence(best_seq)
    return None


def multiplicative_optimize(a0, max_iters, deadline, tracker, seed=1,
                            betas=(5.0, 20.0, 80.0, 300.0), shrink=0.004):
    """
    Exact-J-guided multiplicative mirror descent.
    Maintains nonnegativity and approximate normalization.
    """
    a = clean_sequence(a0)
    if a is None:
        return None
    n = a.size
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    a /= float(a.sum())
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed + 17)
    current = compute_J(a)
    update_tracker(tracker, a)
    if not np.isfinite(current):
        return a

    it_per_beta = max(8, int(max_iters) // max(1, len(betas)))
    ktop = max(8, min(n, int(0.12 * n)))

    for beta in betas:
        for _ in range(it_per_beta):
            if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
                break
            b = fast_conv(a)
            M = float(b.max())
            current = float(2.0 * n * M)
            if not np.isfinite(current):
                break

            # peak weights
            sh = beta * (b - M)
            sh = np.clip(sh, -50.0, 50.0)
            ez = np.exp(sh)
            peak_w = ez / float(ez.sum())

            # violation weights relative to a slightly lower target
            mtgt = (current * (1.0 - shrink)) / (2.0 * n)
            viol = np.maximum(b - mtgt, 0.0)
            sv = float(viol.sum())
            if sv > 0.0:
                viol_w = viol / sv
                w = 0.45 * peak_w + 0.55 * viol_w
            else:
                w = peak_w

            # top-lag mixture to keep support active
            order = np.argsort(b)[::-1]
            top = np.zeros_like(b)
            top[order[:ktop]] = 1.0
            top /= float(top.sum())
            w = 0.65 * w + 0.35 * top
            w = np.maximum(w, 0.0)
            sw = float(w.sum())
            if sw <= 0.0:
                break
            w /= sw

            C = cross_corr_weights_a(w, a)
            C = np.maximum(C, 1e-20)
            base = float(np.median(C))
            if base <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(base):
                break

            accepted = False
            for eta in (0.10, 0.04, 0.2, 0.02, 0.5):
                logf = -eta * (C / base - 1.0)
                logf = np.clip(logf, -9.0, 9.0)
                at = a * np.exp(logf)
                at = np.maximum(at, 1e-14)
                s = float(at.sum())
                if s <= 0.0:
                    continue
                at /= s
                j = compute_J(at)
                if np.isfinite(j) and j < current - 1e-14:
                    a = at
                    current = j
                    accepted = True
                    update_tracker(tracker, a)
                    break
            if not accepted:
                # Tiny smooth noise to escape a flat direction.
                if rng.uniform() < 0.35:
                    at = a + rng.normal(0.0, max(1e-5 / float(n), 1e-9), n)
                    at = np.maximum(at, 1e-14)
                    s = float(at.sum())
                    if s > 0.0:
                        at /= s
                        j = compute_J(at)
                        if np.isfinite(j) and j < current - 1e-14:
                            a = at
                            current = j
                            update_tracker(tracker, a)

    update_tracker(tracker, a)
    return a


def lbfgs_norm_optimize(a0, deadline, tracker,
                        betas=(20.0, 100.0, 500.0),
                        maxiters=(80, 120, 160)):
    """Full-dimensional smooth L-BFGS-B on normalized sequence."""
    a = clean_sequence(a0)
    if a is None:
        return None
    n = a.size
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    a /= float(a.sum())
    update_tracker(tracker, a)
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n

    for beta, iters in zip(betas, maxiters):
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        try:
            res = minimize(
                full_func,
                a,
                jac=full_jac,
                args=(n, beta),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                options={"maxiter": iters, "maxfun": iters * 6,
                         "ftol": 1e-14, "gtol": 1e-7},
            )
            if res is not None and res.x is not None:
                a = np.maximum(res.x, 0.0)
                s = float(a.sum())
                if s > 1e-12:
                    a = a / s
                    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-14)
                    a /= float(a.sum())
                    update_tracker(tracker, a)
        except Exception:
            pass
    return a


def build_A_sparse(a):
    """Build A where (A g)[k] = sum_j a_{k-j} g_j for valid j."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    m = 2 * n - 1
    rows = []
    cols = []
    vals = []
    for k in range(m):
        jl = max(0, k - n + 1)
        jh = min(n - 1, k)
        L = jh - jl + 1
        if L <= 0:
            continue
        rows.extend([k] * L)
        cols.extend(range(jl, jh + 1))
        idx = k - np.arange(jl, jh + 1)
        vals.extend(a[idx].tolist())
    return csc_matrix((vals, (rows, cols)), shape=(m, n))


def lp_best_response(a, M):
    """LP: maximize sum(g) subject to (a*g)_k <= M, g >= 0."""
    a = clean_sequence(a)
    if a is None:
        return None
    n = a.size
    m = 2 * n - 1
    c = -np.ones(n)
    try:
        A = build_A_sparse(a)
        b_ub = np.full(m, float(M))
        bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n
        r = linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method="highs")
        if r.success and r.x is not None:
            g = np.maximum(np.asarray(r.x, dtype=np.float64), 0.0)
            if float(g.sum()) > 1e-12:
                return g
    except Exception:
        pass
    return None


def lp_mirror_descent(a0, max_iters, deadline, tracker):
    """LP best-response Frank-Wolfe / mirror descent refinement."""
    a = clean_sequence(a0)
    if a is None:
        return None
    n = a.size
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    a /= float(a.sum())
    current = compute_J(a)
    update_tracker(tracker, a)
    stall = 0

    for _ in range(int(max_iters)):
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        b = fast_conv(a)
        M = float(b.max())
        g = lp_best_response(a, M)
        if g is None:
            break
        gs = float(g.sum())
        if gs <= 1e-12:
            break
        g = g / gs
        g = np.maximum(g, 1e-14)
        g /= float(g.sum())

        improved = False
        for t in (0.12, 0.04, 0.25, 0.02, 0.45):
            at = (1.0 - t) * a + t * g
            at = np.maximum(at, 1e-14)
            s = float(at.sum())
            if s <= 0.0:
                continue
            at /= s
            j = compute_J(at)
            if np.isfinite(j) and j < current - 1e-14:
                a = at
                current = j
                improved = True
                update_tracker(tracker, a)
                break
        if not improved:
            for t in (0.25, 0.1):
                at = np.power(np.maximum(a, 1e-14), 1.0 - t) * np.power(np.maximum(g, 1e-14), t)
                at = np.maximum(at, 1e-14)
                s = float(at.sum())
                if s <= 0.0:
                    continue
                at /= s
                j = compute_J(at)
                if np.isfinite(j) and j < current - 1e-14:
                    a = at
                    current = j
                    improved = True
                    update_tracker(tracker, a)
                    break
        if improved:
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall >= 5:
                break
    update_tracker(tracker, a)
    return a


def gn_polish(a0, max_iters, deadline, tracker, top_frac=0.25,
              target_shrink=0.008):
    """
    Active-set Gauss-Newton polish: equalize/lower top convolution coefficients.
    Linearizes b_k and solves constrained least squares.
    """
    a = clean_sequence(a0)
    if a is None:
        return None
    n = a.size
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    a /= float(a.sum())
    current = compute_J(a)
    update_tracker(tracker, a)

    L = int(min(n, max(32, int(top_frac * n))))
    shrink = float(target_shrink)
    stall = 0

    for _ in range(int(max_iters)):
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        b = fast_conv(a)
        M = float(b.max())
        if not np.isfinite(M):
            break
        order = np.argsort(b)[::-1]
        S = order[:L]
        target = M * (1.0 - shrink)
        r = b[S] - target

        Jmat = np.zeros((L, n), dtype=np.float64)
        for idx, k in enumerate(S):
            il = max(0, int(k) - n + 1)
            ih = min(n - 1, int(k))
            if il <= ih:
                js = np.arange(il, ih + 1)
                Jmat[idx, il:ih + 1] = 2.0 * a[k - js]

        mu = 8.0
        Aaug = np.empty((L + 1, n), dtype=np.float64)
        Aaug[:L, :] = Jmat
        Aaug[L, :] = mu
        rg = np.empty(L + 1, dtype=np.float64)
        rg[:L] = r
        rg[L] = 0.0

        try:
            dv, _, _, _ = np.linalg.lstsq(Aaug, rg, rcond=None)
            dv = np.nan_to_num(dv, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        except Exception:
            break

        improved = False
        for step in (0.35, 0.15, 0.07, 0.02):
            at = a + step * dv
            at = np.maximum(at, 1e-14)
            s = float(at.sum())
            if s <= 0.0:
                continue
            at /= s
            j = compute_J(at)
            if np.isfinite(j) and j < current - 1e-13:
                a = at
                current = j
                improved = True
                update_tracker(tracker, a)
                break
        if improved:
            stall = 0
            shrink = min(0.035, shrink * 1.15)
        else:
            stall += 1
            shrink = max(1e-4, shrink * 0.45)
            if stall >= 6:
                break
    update_tracker(tracker, a)
    return a


def select_top_sequences(pool, best, max_examples=4, slack=0.04):
    """Return a list of candidate sequences: current best plus close pool items."""
    out = []
    if best is not None:
        out.append(best)
    threshold = tracker_score + float(slack)  # avoid global? Use best_score.
    return out


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1500):
    """
    Main search driver. Returns best nonnegative coefficient list found.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget_s = max(120.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + budget_s - 22.0
    tracker = {"best": None, "score": float("inf"), "t0": t0}
    pool = []

    parent = None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if p.ndim == 1 and p.size > 0:
            pc = clean_sequence(p)
            if pc is not None:
                parent = pc
                update_tracker(tracker, parent)
                pool_add(pool, parent, max_size=16, tracker=tracker)
    except Exception:
        pass

    print(f"Starting search budget={budget_s:.0f}s", flush=True)

    # Candidate lengths.
    lengths = [512, 768, 1024]
    if parent is not None:
        np_len = int(parent.size)
        if 64 <= np_len <= 2048 and np_len not in lengths:
            lengths.append(np_len)
    lengths = sorted(set(lengths))

    # Add resampled parent/uniform initializations.
    for n in lengths:
        if parent is not None:
            a = resample_seq(parent, n)
            if a is not None:
                pool_add(pool, a, max_size=16, tracker=tracker)
        else:
            pool_add(pool, np.ones(n) / float(n), max_size=16, tracker=tracker)

    # Phase 1: flexible spline search.
    for n in lengths:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 350.0:
            break
        K = 40 if n <= 768 else 48
        max_calls = 6 if n <= 768 else 4
        seq = spline_param_search(
            n,
            seed=seed + 1000 + n,
            parent_seq=parent,
            K=K,
            deadline=deadline - 350.0,
            max_calls=max_calls,
        )
        if seq is not None:
            pool_add(pool, seq, max_size=16, tracker=tracker)

    # Phase 2: one harmonic constructions.
    for n in lengths[:3]:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 300.0:
            break
        seq = one_harmonic_search(
            n,
            seed=seed + 2000 + n,
            deadline=deadline - 300.0,
            m_list=(1, 2, 3, 4),
            a_count=18,
            phi_count=48,
        )
        if seq is not None:
            pool_add(pool, seq, max_size=16, tracker=tracker)

    # Phase 3: multiplicative refinement of diverse candidates.
    # Reserve time for later expensive LP/polish.
    for idx in range(len(pool)):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 320.0:
            break
        sc, a = pool[idx]
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            continue
        max_iters = 420 if a.size <= 768 else 220
        a2 = multiplicative_optimize(
            a,
            max_iters=max_iters,
            deadline=deadline - 320.0,
            tracker=tracker,
            seed=seed + 3100 + idx,
        )
        if a2 is not None:
            pool_add(pool, a2, max_size=16, tracker=tracker)
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 320.0:
            break

    # Phase 4: full-dimensional smooth L-BFGS on current best and near-best pool.
    if tracker.get("best") is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 260.0:
        top_seqs = []
        top_seqs.append(tracker["best"])
        best_score = tracker["score"]
        for sc, a in pool:
            if len(top_seqs) >= 4:
                break
            if a is not tracker["best"] and np.isfinite(sc) and sc <= best_score + 0.035:
                top_seqs.append(a)

        for i, a in enumerate(top_seqs):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 260.0:
                break
            if a is None:
                continue
            a2 = lbfgs_norm_optimize(
                a,
                deadline=deadline - 260.0,
                tracker=tracker,
                betas=(20.0, 100.0, 500.0),
                maxiters=(70, 110, 150),
            )
            if a2 is not None:
                pool_add(pool, a2, max_size=16, tracker=tracker)

    # Phase 5: LP mirror descent / Frank-Wolfe on best and near-best.
    if tracker.get("best") is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 180.0:
        top_seqs = []
        top_seqs.append(tracker["best"])
        best_score = tracker["score"]
        added = 0
        for sc, a in pool:
            if added >= 2:
                break
            if a is not tracker["best"] and np.isfinite(sc) and sc <= best_score + 0.06:
                top_seqs.append(a)
                added += 1

        for a in top_seqs:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 120.0:
                break
            if a is None:
                continue
            n = a.size
            max_iter = 420 if n <= 640 else 260
            a2 = lp_mirror_descent(a, max_iters=max_iter,
                                   deadline=deadline - 100.0, tracker=tracker)
            if a2 is not None:
                pool_add(pool, a2, max_size=16, tracker=tracker)

    # Phase 6: final polishing loop on current best.
    if tracker.get("best") is not None:
        # GN polish first, then multiplicative, then short LP, repeating if time allows.
        cycle = 0
        while time.monotonic() < deadline - 45.0 and cycle < 12:
            cycle += 1
            a = tracker["best"]
            if a is None:
                break

            if time.monotonic() < deadline - 170.0 and a.size <= 1536:
                a2 = gn_polish(a, max_iters=70, deadline=deadline - 150.0, tracker=tracker)
            elif time.monotonic() < deadline - 90.0:
                a2 = multiplicative_optimize(
                    a,
                    max_iters=120,
                    deadline=deadline - 90.0,
                    tracker=tracker,
                    seed=seed + 4000 + cycle,
                    betas=(80.0, 300.0, 1200.0),
                    shrink=0.002,
                )
            else:
                break

            if a2 is not None:
                pool_add(pool, a2, max_size=16, tracker=tracker)

            # Occasional LP if sequence length moderate.
            if time.monotonic() < deadline - 70.0 and tracker.get("best") is not None and tracker["best"].size <= 1024:
                a3 = lp_mirror_descent(tracker["best"], max_iters=60,
                                       deadline=deadline - 55.0, tracker=tracker)
                if a3 is not None:
                    pool_add(pool, a3, max_size=16, tracker=tracker)

    # Phase 7: if enough time remains, try larger lengths by resampling best.
    if tracker.get("best") is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 180.0:
        for n_large in (1536, 2048):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 90.0:
                break
            a = resample_seq(tracker["best"], n_large)
            if a is None:
                continue
            a = multiplicative_optimize(
                a,
                max_iters=180,
                deadline=deadline - 90.0,
                tracker=tracker,
                seed=seed + 5000 + n_large,
                betas=(20.0, 80.0, 300.0),
            )
            if a is not None:
                a = gn_polish(a, max_iters=45, deadline=deadline - 60.0, tracker=tracker)
            if a is not None:
                pool_add(pool, a, max_size=16, tracker=tracker)

    # Final validation and return.
    best = tracker.get("best")
    if best is None:
        # Very conservative fallback.
        best = np.ones(512, dtype=np.float64) / 512.0
        compute_J(best)

    best = clean_sequence(best)
    if best is None:
        best = np.ones(128, dtype=np.float64) / 128.0

    best = np.clip(best, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(best.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        best = np.ones(512, dtype=np.float64) / 512.0

    final_score = compute_J(best)
    if not np.isfinite(final_score):
        best = np.ones(512, dtype=np.float64) / 512.0
        final_score = compute_J(best)

    print(f"Final returned: J={final_score:.9f} n={best.size} "
          f"t={time.monotonic()-t0:.1f}s", flush=True)

    return [float(x) for x in best]
```
